parent
5e946d5b4f8355a928acdd857b6dd4571ec144d7 [ diff ] 
gpu: Flush active/pending queries on virtual context switch and FBO change

When flush_queries_before_deleting_or_unbinding_fbo is enabled (ARM Mali
Valhall/Avalon), a user FBO must not be unbound or deleted while GPU
jobs or queries referencing it remain unflushed.

Previously:
1. GLES2DecoderImpl::FlushQueriesBeforeDeletingOrUnbindingFboWorkaround
   only checked HasPendingQueries(), missing queries that had been begun
   (in active_queries_) but not yet ended when the FBO was unbound or
   deleted.
2. With virtualized GL contexts, switching out a decoder's virtual
   context allows the state restorer, Skia (whose workaround only tracks
   Skia-issued timer queries), or another decoder on the same real GL
   context to rebind or delete FBOs while the outgoing decoder still has
   unflushed queries.

Fix both by:
- Adding QueryManager::HaveActiveQueries() and checking both active and
  pending queries in FlushQueriesBeforeDeletingOrUnbindingFboWorkaround.
- Overriding PauseQueries() in GLES2QueryManager to flush the query job
  chain when switching out a virtual context that had active or pending
  queries.

Assisted-by: Gemini 4 Argon (Medium)

Classify //chrome/browser:* small targets' deps vs public_deps

Rule: headers in `public`, their deps in `public_deps`; .cc files in
`sources`, their deps in `deps`. Applied to the ~43 small targets
declared between lines 115 and 765:

- 22 header-only targets (.cc lives in :core): deps -> public_deps
  wholesale, since the single header needs everything.
- 7 mixed targets got per-dep reclassification based on actually
  reading each file's #includes: browser_process, global_features,
  browser_process_platform_part, command_updater_impl,
  file_util_service, icon_loader.
- 2 pure-.cc targets (no public header) correctly left alone.

09afd8922e7e7c01b002df82d8256e967d1e8eb5
parent
0bb60519a5287c1c0b29444426284aa52883c552 [ diff ] 
[Android/TabUI] Avoid O(N^2) ModelList scans in resetWithListOfTabs

During TabListMediator.resetWithListOfTabs with N tabs,
addObserversForTab linearly scans mModelList via getModelFromTabId and
calls updateActorUiState for every tab right before resetWithListOfTabs
immediately updates or rebuilds every tab's PropertyModel. Furthermore,
on warm opens where areTabsUnchanged(tabs) is true, the in-place update
loop calls mModelList.indexOfNthTabCard(i) for each tab, scanning from
index 0 on every iteration in O(N^2) time.

Skip the redundant getModelFromTabId and updateActorUiState pass inside
addObserversForTab during batch resets, and replace the
indexOfNthTabCard(i) loop with a single O(N) cursor pass over
mModelList.

Fixed: 579505319

176bca626094eb84580da1e61f32a8c1e7bb0434
parent
a13bd77b555727c8786cce4b1ee8c13e26cf41b0 [ diff ] 
[Android/TabUI] Hoist PriceTracking JNI & skip extra FaviconFetcher

During TabListMediator.resetWithListOfTabs with N tabs,
setupPersistedTabDataFetcherForTab queries
PriceTrackingUtilities.isTrackPricesOnTabsEnabled(mOriginalProfile) on
every tab even though mOriginalProfile is invariant across the batch
reset, resulting in 3 * N redundant native JNI calls. Additionally,
addTabInfoToModelForTab unconditionally assigns a default
FAVICON_FETCHER that is immediately overwritten by updateFaviconForTab
when TabListFaviconProvider is already initialized.

Lazily cache isTrackPricesOnTabsEnabled(mOriginalProfile) once per
batch reset pass and skip setting the default FAVICON_FETCHER when
TabListFaviconProvider is initialized.

Bug: 579505319

[Autofill] Re-enable `NoLongerVisibleBothNoActions` on Mac

`FormSubmissionTrackerTest.NoLongerVisibleBothNoActions` was originally
disabled on macOS before `FormSubmissionTrackerTest` was updated in
https://crrev.com/c/6198229 to wait on a `base::RunLoop` quit by the
mock `AutofillDriver::FormSubmitted()` expectation instead of relying on
`base::RunLoop().RunUntilIdle()`.

Remove the `#if BUILDFLAG(IS_MAC)` disablement so the test runs on
macOS as well.

Verified on macOS and Linux.

Part of the AI Week project (https://docs.google.com/document/d/1cM_wab8WXBAwhI-fIlcNYOrozXssqg_wbqd0IrhW7DI/edit, http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome).

Bug: 40559394

82e3781e009ceb63c9f5eb3740492751af9f5bc0 [ diff ] 
[Signin] Add a struct for the names of one AI subscription tier

Adds AiTierName, which holds the short and full name of one AI
subscription tier, in a header of its own so that the branded names in
src-internal can use it too.

Nothing uses it yet. This is the first step of replacing
kAiTierShortNames and kAiTierFullNames with a single map, kAiTierNames,
landed in an order where every combination of the public and private
repos keeps compiling:
1. This CL.
2. Private: add kAiTierNames next to the old maps.
3. Public: use kAiTierNames, with the DEPS roll to step 2.
4. Private: remove the old maps.

omnibox: Move a WebUI popup's WebContents to another window

Follow-me popups share one classic and one AI Mode popup per profile
across browser windows. Add the presenter API that moves a loaded popup
WebContents from one window to another.

OmniboxPopupPresenterBase::ReleaseWebUIContents() takes the WebContents
out of a hidden popup: the content view detaches the WebUI handlers
from its window (OmniboxPopupUI::DetachFromWindow()) and gives up the
WebContents, and the presenter destroys the view, so the window is back
to having no popup content. AdoptWebUIContents() on another window's
presenter builds a new content view around that WebContents through
CreateWebUIContent(), which now takes an optional WebContents to adopt.

The content view connects an adopted WebContents to its window the
same way as a newly loaded one (host, browser window, tab selection
listener, omnibox controller, focus callback), sets its color provider
source to the new window, sets the presenter delegate and calls
OmniboxPopupUI::AttachToWindow(). The shared steps move out of
LoadContent() into BindContentsToWindow().

Nothing calls the new API outside tests yet. The registry and the move
policy come next, behind a flag.

omnibox: Let the WebUI popup's handlers move to another window

Each browser window creates its own classic and AI Mode popup
WebContents. To share one popup of each kind per profile, a popup's
WebContents has to move between windows, and its Mojo handlers are
bound to the omnibox, tabs and contextual session of the window that
created it.

Add OmniboxPopupUI::DetachFromWindow() and AttachToWindow(). Detach
runs while the old window still embeds the popup: the handlers stop
observing its omnibox controller, edit model, tab list and tabs, drop
tab underlines, pending tab context, result snapshots, the Drive picker
and the input state model of its active tab, and the contextual
session, which holds context from the old window, is cleared. The AI
Mode handler also stops its own autocomplete. Afterwards nothing points
into the old window, so it can close before the popup is attached
elsewhere. Attach rebinds the handlers to the new window's omnibox
controller and tabs and, if the new window has results, sends them
and its popup selection to the page.

Nothing calls these yet. The views layer will move the WebContents
between windows behind a flag in a follow-up. The full WebUI popup is
not supported.

gpu: Disallow SHARED_IMAGE_USAGE_MIPMAP from client requests

Move SHARED_IMAGE_USAGE_MIPMAP to service-side-only usages (after
LAST_CLIENT_USAGE), as it is only used on the GPU side by Viz's
SkiaRenderer for render pass backings. Also validate
IsValidClientUsage() in SharedImageStub so invalid or service-only
client usages are rejected over IPC.

capture_mode: Skip disabled menu items in AppendHighlightableItems
📋 Metadata
commit
870a4230e6a8a52b4b61a219f6394b3d318dcd3f
[ log ]
author
Sergey Poromov <poromov@google.com>
Sat Oct 31 08:27:26 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 31 08:27:26 2026
tree
f6b55fc1627429eb3f985d0163baa4801789dfe0
parent
34b69cd5763717386be238e7ce7eb92c47cf4d7a [ diff ] 
capture_mode: Skip disabled menu items in AppendHighlightableItems

In CaptureModeMenuGroup::AppendHighlightableItems(), options_ were
filtered with option->GetEnabled() before being added to
highlightable_items, whereas menu_items_ were appended unconditionally.
When ScreenCaptureLocation policy enforcement is active, the "Select
folder..." CaptureModeMenuItem is disabled, yet it remained in the list
of highlightable items for keyboard focus cycling.

Filter menu_items_ by menu_item->GetEnabled() in
CaptureModeMenuGroup::AppendHighlightableItems() to match its documented
contract and prevent keyboard navigation from focusing disabled menu
items. Update CaptureModeSettingsTest.FolderSetByPolicy to verify that
disabled options and menu items are excluded from highlightable items.

a79758bcf708cf5798b8315b6c85158382397925
parent
b63c0cf33ffc2218d76b2ebf7bafaed2a2619ed2 [ diff ] 
Skip unused warmup in the default inline script cache hint test

DefaultCacheHint runs an expensive warmup script on its initial navigation
and every reload, even when caching for default hints is disabled. This
unnecessary work can exhaust the test timeout on slow configurations.

Skip the warmup only for default hints with caching disabled. Preserve
the initial navigation, all five reload attempts, and the assertion that
no cache is produced. Also assert that no inline script cache fetch is
attempted across these navigations, so a cold or uninitialized backend
cannot hide accidental cache access.

Bug: 579544710
Test: Not run (no native Chromium build available).

tree
6d71ddaff375812128c3230131307ac23492492b
parent
ccc47941baeef88a702f75413f72a19dbd20ff1b [ diff ] 
Make input handler event metrics tests independent of uptime

The event timestamps are fixed, but the frame clock starts at system
uptime. With low uptime and UpdateScrollPredictorInputMapping enabled,
events can remain queued and leave saved_metrics empty.

Initialize the frame clock one second after the fixed event timestamp.
Assert the metrics list size before indexing it to avoid out-of-bounds
access when metrics are missing.

Bug: None

Reset Smart Tab Sharing when dismissing or clearing composebox

Resets the Smart Tab Sharing (STS) state via the backend handler when
the composebox is closed without submitting or when all inputs are
cleared outside of a submission. Also adds resetSmartTabSharing to the
New Tab Page searchbox to reset STS state when the composebox is
dismissed. Includes corresponding WebUI unit tests.

Respect context sharing settings in Contextual Tasks eligibility

Updates IsTabSharingEligible to check whether context sharing is enabled in preferences via ContextualSearchService::IsContextSharingEnabled.

Additionally, EntryPointEligibilityManager now observes the kSearchContentSharingSettings preference so that listeners are notified when eligibility changes as a result of pref updates.

Remove unused adaptation OptimizationTargets and use kFeatureNotEnabled

Reserve the unused model execution OptimizationTarget enum values in
models.proto (OPTIMIZATION_TARGET_COMPOSE and
OPTIMIZATION_TARGET_MODEL_EXECUTION_FEATURE_*) and remove their entries
from GetStringNameForOptimizationTarget() and histograms.xml. Marking
them reserved allows PredictionModelStore to clean up stale adaptation
directories on disk.

Return kFeatureNotEnabled instead of kModelAdaptationNotAvailable from
ModelBrokerAndroid::SolutionFactory::MakeSolution() when
IsModelAllowed() returns false.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Removed OptimizationTarget variants for retired on-device model adaptation targets.

Reland "Reland "ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions""

This is a reland of commit ff9c8d4945e0d6baab644fa395cf812e9c2ce44d.
Diff is in https://crrev.com/c/8687765/1..2; it also disables the
paint stability timeout for ActorSkipObservationDelayTest.

Verified with 100 runs on the previously failing trybot (using UTR).

Original change's description:
> Reland "ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions"
>
> This is a reland of commit 51de1aad9a337fd7a7e45bac037a5338baec4ab2.
> Diff is in https://crrev.com/c/8669854/1..2.
>
> Original change's description:
> > ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions
> >
> > TTC doesn't take an observation after performing an action; page context
> > is delivered separately by TtcPageContextMonitor. Waiting for the page
> > to settle (ObservationDelayController) therefore only delays the tool
> > response to the model, by up to 10s on busy pages.
> >
> > Add ToolRequest::SetSkipObservationDelay(), which makes
> > actor::ToolController complete a tool as soon as it finishes invoking
> > without creating an ObservationDelayController. Follow-up actions
> > enqueued by a tool (e.g. OpenUrlTool's NavigateTool) inherit the flag
> > from the action that enqueued them. TTC sets it on every action it
> > performs.
> >
> > Bug: 571644190
> > Change-Id: I811fd7c91cf04ec183caf5db07902a9218811729
> > Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8659320
> > Reviewed-by: David Bokan <bokan@chromium.org>
> > Commit-Queue: Adithya Srinivasan <adithyas@chromium.org>
> > Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1729054}
>
> Bug: 571644190

46b7a694117923ffc35965b1e1ac405d3fcc9ca2 [ diff ] 
Shrink ListedElement by 16 bytes

Narrow ListedElement's two enums to uint8_t and declare the one-byte
fields before the Member and String fields, so they fill the padding
after the vtable pointer.

sizeof(ListedElement):
  64-bit, compressed Member (x64/arm64): 48 -> 32 bytes
  64-bit, uncompressed Member:           56 -> 48 bytes
  32-bit (arm/x86):                      36 -> 24 bytes

Form controls, HTMLObjectElement and form-associated ElementInternals
shrink by the same amount. Add ASSERT_SIZE to keep it from regressing.

No behavior change.

tree
046505aa13300b745b26376238209d9e3681509a
parent
c309c767bb35b9b3e83cd99dbb0f22e256b8b654 [ diff ] 
BidirectionalStream: Don't save alt-service information with bad cert.

When the user allows an invalid cert for a BidirectionalStream, and the
connection tries to provide alt-service information, ignore the alt
service information. This matches behavior elsewhere (e.g., in
HttpNetworkTransaction::ProcessAltSvcHeader()).

The reason for this is that this information is persisted, so a single
server allowed to use a bad cert once, could create an alt service
entry that would persist for years. While alt-service entries do not
bypass cert validity checks, we'd still try to talk to the alt service
location every time a user connects to a server, which would not be
great.

perftests, vulkan test support, and gl unit tests by moving

targets while keeping the suppression on cc_unittests.

a491f8ac27bb20a7f7a3098c7d2f3c4c63cf6728
parent
08cd912acccaf5bf14cbd26767ce9352359b58e7 [ diff ] 
Extensions: Use JniRawPtr in NativeMessagePort

Replace the raw long mNativePtr field in NativeMessageAndroidPort with
JniRawPtr so JNI Zero tracks the borrowed reference to
::extensions::NativeMessageAndroidPort with BackupRefPtr protection
across JNI calls.

Pass jni_zero::MakeRaw(this) when constructing the Java peer and invoke
JniRawPtr.release() inside destroy() when the C++ owner detaches so
stale native invocations fail safely.

tree
eb2151b9ca339237d280e3e2d228b726471ae94e
parent
2b0cfb77d79f37f5c1bac2498eafc9855f633284 [ diff ] 
[Android] Allow dropping an image dragged from this app

Since the ClipboardConfusedDeputyDefenseFiles defense, EventForwarder
rejects dropped content URIs served by this app. Dragging an image puts
a URI of this app's DropDataContentProvider on the drag, so an image
dragged from one tab could no longer be dropped into another.

A new EventForwarder.isUriSafeForDrop(), similar to
ClipboardImpl.isUriSafeForRead(), now allows URIs of this app's
DropDataProvider, only when they resolve to a non-exported provider of
this app, and still rejects any other content URI of this app.
DropDataProvider only serves the image that this app is dragging, and
only until shortly after the drag ends.

Both Uri.getAuthority() and the authority of the string form must be
DropDataProvider's, since on Android Q a Uri from another app's parcel
can have a getAuthority() which differs from its string form, and the
string form is what is later opened.

tree
1d17f5e043270709304961a62a60ae28b4a2751b
parent
73d0cb338e530bf0686db4ab2e9b3c3a12fc9a28 [ diff ] 
WebView: Destroy popup native if receiver is gone

When the AwContents that should host a popup has already been
destroyed, receivePopupContents() returned without deleting the native
AwContents it was handed. The opener gave up ownership in
releasePopupAwContents(), so nothing else deletes it, and the native
AwContents leaks together with its WebContents.

Delete it in that case, as supplyContentsForPopup() already does when
the app supplies no AwContents for the popup. The new PopupWindowTest
case fails without this change: the native instance count stays one
higher than expected.

This is also needed before AwContents can be owned through
JniUniquePtr, whose LifetimeAssert fails when a JniUniquePtr is
garbage collected without destroy().

[PA] Skip ThreadCache poisoning for zapped SchedulerLoopQuarantine slots

`ThreadCache::PutInBucket()` poisons the remainder of the first 64-byte
cache line with `0xbadbad00` (`HAS_FREELIST_SHADOW_ENTRY` on x86_64)
before writing the freelist entry. In `perf annotate` of
`SchedulerLoopQuarantinePerfTest` (`thinlto_optimize_max`), this
poisoning loop accounts for 32.4% of CPU samples in
`SchedulerLoopQuarantineBranch::PurgeInternal()`. When
`enable_zapping_` is true (as in `browser_main` and
`browser_main_unlimited`), `Quarantine()` already zaps the entire
usable slot with `kFreedByte` (`0xcd`) upon entry to the quarantine,
making the second poisoning pass in `PutInBucket()` redundant.

This CL templates `ThreadCache::MaybePutInCache()` and
`ThreadCache::PutInBucket()` on `bool poison = true`, and calls
`MaybePutInCache<false>()` from `ReleaseDequarantinedSlot()` when
`enable_zapping_` is true.

SchedulerLoopQuarantinePerfTest results on Pinpoint ("all lines" rows
read every cache line of each object before freeing it, like a
destructor that reads all fields):

Significance: ◆ p < 0.05, ◆◆ p < 0.01, ◆◆◆ p < 0.001

Windows (win-11-perf, 30 runs), config: browser_main - https://pinpoint-dot-chromeperf.appspot.com/job/11aa2d3b190000

- Positive impact: On Windows (x86_64), reduces time by 3.8%-6.3% across
  all `storm_*` benchmarks and by 7.6%-8.0% on `steady_churn`
  (`browser_main`).
- No impact: On Windows `steady_churn` (`unlimited`), `RunSteadyChurn()`
  stops its timer before the end-of-task purge, so no slots are
  dequarantined during the timed window. On Mac (ARM64),
  `PutInBucket()`'s poisoning loop is guarded by
  `PA_BUILDFLAG(PA_ARCH_CPU_X86_64)`, so codegen is unchanged.
- Negative impact: None.

and `printing::FormatDocumentTitleWithOwnerAndLength()` was removed in

[Reading mode] Shift cached line focus bounds on word boundaries

Shift cached text bounds instead of re-measuring the DOM when scrolling
during Read Aloud word boundaries.

- Latency: Avoids recomputing rects for the entire document when speech
  scrolls.
- Bug fixes: Syncs text bounds before matching the spoken word's line if
  speech scrolled prior to `onWordBoundary()`, and shifts the
  cursor-mode focal point when instant recentering scrolls.

parent
b6824f1a37e04b40b42a9a0807e068783b4afdc8 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Add Android.Omnibox.SuggestionList.KeystrokeToUiModel.{First,Last}

The existing
Android.Omnibox.SuggestionList.RequestToUiModel.{First,Last} histograms
start their clock when the autocomplete request is issued, which happens
inside the deferred action scheduled by
AutocompleteMediator.onTextChanged - i.e. after the 30ms
OMNIBOX_SUGGESTION_START_DELAY_MS has already elapsed. They therefore
exclude the deferral between the keystroke and the request start, and
cannot be used to evaluate changes to that deferral (crbug/578525544).
Android currently has no keystroke-anchored suggestion latency metric.

This CL adds KeystrokeToUiModel.{First,Last}, which measure from the
keystroke (captured synchronously in onTextChanged) to the same end
points as RequestToUiModel, with the same bucketing and cancel semantics
so the two are directly comparable: a refining keystroke or ending the
interaction drops the pending .Last. The new histograms are only
recorded for typed-text requests, not zero-prefix requests.
RequestToUiModel is left unchanged to preserve longitudinal
comparability.

Bug: 578525544

parent
e28940e01e2826de58d938c6b097b57c495dca92 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Lazily create ActionChipsView; reuse chip adapters

Previously, every BaseSuggestionView eagerly constructed and attached an
ActionChipsView (a RecyclerView + LinearLayoutManager) in its
constructor, even though most suggestions do not have action chips.
Additionally, BaseSuggestionViewBinder called ActionChipsBinder.bind()
for every suggestion property key, and ActionChipsBinder allocated a new
SimpleRecyclerViewAdapter on every bind of ACTION_CHIPS even when the
bound ModelList had not changed.

This change:
1. Defers ActionChipsView instantiation in BaseSuggestionView to
   ensureActionChipsView(), only creating and attaching it when
   BaseSuggestionViewProperties.ACTION_CHIPS is non-null. ACTION_CHIPS
   moves from ActionChipsProperties to the containing view's
   properties and ActionChipsProperties is removed.
2. Binds ACTION_CHIPS and DEVICE_FORM_FACTOR directly in
   BaseSuggestionViewBinder (ActionChipsBinder is removed), skipping
   the carousel entirely when ACTION_CHIPS is null and no
   ActionChipsView exists. BaseSuggestionView caches lead-in spacing
   and fading-edge settings so a lazily created carousel picks them up.
3. Has BaseSuggestionViewBinder manage the carousel adapter: the attached
   adapter is reused when it already presents the suggestion's chip
   list, swapped (via swapAdapter, so the RecycledViewPool survives)
   when the list changes, and detached when chips are cleared.
   ActionChipsView holds no model state and never sees the ModelList.
4. Makes chip theming a property of the chip model. ActionChipProperties
   (which also hosts the carousel ViewType) extends ChipProperties with
   RESOURCE_PROVIDER; ActionChipsProcessor
   sets it, and ActionChipViewBinder applies the cached scheme-dependent
   background tint on bind. This replaces the incognito theme overlay
   (whose only effective difference was the chip background color) and
   lets recycled ChipViews be themed on rebind instead of re-inflated.
5. Avoids redundant SpacingRecyclerViewItemDecoration replacement in
   ActionChipsView.setLeadInSpacing() when spacing is unchanged.

Bug: 575959164

[Extensions] Skip verification for built-in component extensions

Built-in component extensions (mojom::ManifestLocation::kComponent) do
not ship content verification hashes and are not managed by
ExternalProviderManager. Return Mode::NONE for kComponent in
ChromeContentVerifierDelegate::GetVerifyInfo().

Previously, GetVerifyInfo() enabled verification and repair for
kComponent extensions (via MustRemainEnabled()). Skipping kComponent
avoids at login on ChromeOS (~9.2 MiB idle PSS savings):
- Initializing the default StoragePartition and NetworkContext to
  fetch signed hashes for kComponent extensions with a webstore
  update_url.
- Triggering CorruptedExtensionReinstaller on missing hashes, which
  spawns DataDecoderService via ExtensionDownloader to parse update
  manifest XML.

Bug: 40625642, b:575247202

[MiraclePtr] Enforce raw_ptr in containers under chrome/browser/ttc

Replace raw pointers (`content::WebContents*`) with
`raw_ptr<content::WebContents>` in `RecordingObserver::changes_` and
`TakeChanges()` across `voice_focused_contents_tracker` tests.

This advances MiraclePtr's expansion to containers and preempts an
upcoming clang-plugin update that will strictly ban these un-rewritten
instances.

Bug: b:501142609
Doc: go/miracleptr-in-containers

Return base::span from ExtensionsToolbarViewModel's action ID getters

Update GetAllActionIds() and GetPinnedActionIds() in
ExtensionsToolbarViewModel and ExtensionsToolbarAndroid to return
base::span instead of container references or vectors. This avoids
copying vectors for Android JNI bindings and eliminates static empty
fallback containers.

parent
58cdf601764b815a4af3886566b9fd951c5b2188 [ diff ] 
Attribute zero-state opens to correct pin buttons

When opening the contextual tasks side panel in zero state (for a new
thread) from a pinned button, Show() cannot infer the entry source.
Previously, std::nullopt was passed, causing the entry source to fall
back to kOther.

This explicitly passes kPermanentToolbarButton as the entry source so
metrics for session duration and panel closes are correctly recorded
alongside continue-thread opens.

Bug: b:580017132

parent
3f4971126ff7cf0236032b6c6f52cf37a4487683 [ diff ] 
[Reading mode PDFs] Remap annotations when splitting text runs

Links, highlights, form graphics, and images refer to text runs by
index. Splitting a run shifts the index of every later run, so without
remapping, these annotations would attach to the wrong text.

`pdf::SplitTextRunsAtVerticalDividers()` now also returns the index of
each original run's first part. `pdf::RemapTextRunIndices()` uses it to
remap link, highlight, form graphic, and image text run indices. The
`PdfAccessibilityTreeBuilder` constructor copies and remaps the
annotations only when a run is split.

Bug: 574033561
